Tools pre-approved by `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/settings-reference#permission-settings`,children:`permission rules`}),` run, and so do calls that need no approval in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`default`}),` mode, such as file reads inside your working directories. Every call that would otherwise prompt is denied, though a `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/plugins/mods/overview`,children:`mod`}),` you installed can still approve `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/permissions#precedence-among-rules-hooks-and-mods`,children:`some of those calls`}),`. A few calls, such as `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/permissions#network-paths`,children:`reads from network paths`}),`, are denied even if you’ve allowed them. For the list, see `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/permissions#don%E2%80%99t-ask-mode-dontask`,children:`Don’t ask mode`})]}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Headless agents with nobody to approve calls`})]}),_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`\"auto\"`})}),_jsxs(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:[`Uses a model classifier to review actions such as shell commands and network requests, allowing or blocking each one it reviews. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/permission-modes#eliminate-prompts-with-auto-mode`,children:`Auto mode`}),` for availability and the decision order`]}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Autonomous agents that still want safety guardrails on tool use`})]}),_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`\"bypassPermissions\"`})}),_jsxs(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:[`Runs tool calls without asking for approval, including calls to tools you didn’t list in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`allowedTools`}),`. A few calls still go to your `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`canUseTool`}),` callback, listed under `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/permissions#bypass-permissions-mode-bypasspermissions`,children:`Bypass permissions mode`}),`. In the TypeScript SDK, also requires `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`allowDangerouslySkipPermissions: true`}),` in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`options`}),`. Can’t be used when running as root on Unix. Use only in isolated environments where the agent’s actions can’t affect systems you care about`]}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`CI, containers, or other isolated environments`})]})]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`For interactive applications, use `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`\"default\"`}),` with a tool approval callback to surface approval prompts. For agents that edit files on a dev machine, use `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`\"acceptEdits\"`}),`. Reserve `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`\"bypassPermissions\"`}),` for CI, containers, or other isolated environments. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/permissions`,children:`Permissions`}),` for full details.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`model`,children:`Model`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`Set the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`model`}),` option to choose which model runs the session. For more information, see `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/configuration#choose-a-model`,children:`Choose a model`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`the-context-window`,children:`The context window`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`The context window is the total amount of information available to Claude during a session. It does not reset between turns within a session. Everything accumulates: the system prompt, tool definitions, conversation history, tool inputs, and tool outputs. Content that stays the same across turns (system prompt, tool definitions, CLAUDE.md) is automatically `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/prompt-caching`,children:`prompt cached`}),`, which reduces cost and latency for repeated prefixes. For how a custom system prompt or `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`append`}),` text affects cache reuse across sessions, see `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/modifying-system-prompts#improve-prompt-caching-across-users-and-machines`,children:`Modifying system prompts`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`what-consumes-context`,children:`What consumes context`}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Here’s how each component affects context in the SDK:`}),`\n`,_jsxs(Table,{children:[_jsx(_components.thead,{children:_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsx(_components.th,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Source`}),_jsx(_components.th,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`When it loads`}),_jsx(_components.th,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Impact`})]})}),_jsxs(_components.tbody,{children:[_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`System prompt`})}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Every request`}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Small fixed cost, always present`})]}),_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`CLAUDE.md files`})}),_jsxs(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:[`Session start, via `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/claude-code-features`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`settingSources`})})]}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Full content in every request (but prompt-cached)`})]}),_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Tool definitions`})}),_jsxs(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:[`Every request for core built-in tools such as Bash, Read, and Edit; on demand for MCP tools and other built-in tools, which `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/mcp#mcp-tool-search`,children:`tool search`}),` defers by default`]}),_jsxs(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:[`Every schema loads upfront in the cases under `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/tool-search#configure-tool-search`,children:`Configure tool search`})]})]}),_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Conversation history`})}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Accumulates over turns`}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Grows with each turn: prompts, responses, tool inputs, tool outputs`})]}),_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Skill descriptions`})}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Session start, via setting sources`}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Short summaries; full content loads only when invoked`})]})]})]}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Large tool outputs consume significant context. Reading a big file or running a command with verbose output can use thousands of tokens in a single turn. Context accumulates across turns, so longer sessions with many tool calls build up significantly more context than short ones.`}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`automatic-compaction`,children:`Automatic compaction`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`When the context window approaches its limit, the SDK automatically compacts the conversation: it summarizes older history to free space, keeping your most recent exchanges and key decisions intact. The SDK emits a message with `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`type: \"system\"`}),` and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`subtype: \"compact_boundary\"`}),` in the stream when this happens (in Python this is a `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`SystemMessage`}),`; in TypeScript it is a separate `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`SDKCompactBoundaryMessage`}),` type).`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`Compaction replaces older messages with a summary, so specific instructions from early in the conversation may not be preserved. Persistent rules belong in CLAUDE.md (loaded via `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/claude-code-features`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`settingSources`})}),`) rather than in the initial prompt, because CLAUDE.md content is re-injected on every request.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`You can customize compaction behavior in several ways:`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Summarization instructions in CLAUDE.md:`}),` The compactor reads your CLAUDE.md like any other context, so you can include a section telling it what to preserve when summarizing. The compactor matches on intent, so the section header is free-form.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`PreCompact`}),` hook:`]}),` Run custom logic before compaction occurs, for example to archive the full transcript. The hook receives a `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`trigger`}),` field (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`manual`}),` or `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`auto`}),`). See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/hooks`,children:`hooks`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Manual compaction:`}),` Send `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`/compact`}),` as a prompt string to trigger compaction on demand. Commands sent this way are ordinary SDK inputs. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/skills#dispatch-commands-by-name`,children:`dispatch commands by name`}),`.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Accordion,{title:`Example: Summarization instructions in CLAUDE.md`,id:`example-summarization-instructions-in-claude-md`,children:[_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Add a section to your project’s CLAUDE.md telling the compactor what to preserve. strategies for long-running agents:`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Use subagents for subtasks.`}),` A subagent returns only its final response, so your main agent’s context grows by a summary, not by the subtask’s transcript.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Be selective with tools.`}),` Every tool definition takes context space. Use the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`tools`}),` field on `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/subagents#agentdefinition-configuration`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`AgentDefinition`})}),` to scope subagents to the minimum set they need.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Watch MCP server costs.`}),` `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/mcp#mcp-tool-search`,children:`MCP tool search`}),` defers MCP tool schemas by default and loads them on demand. When tool search is off or has fallen back to upfront loading, each MCP server adds all its tool schemas to every request, so a few servers with many tools can consume significant context before the agent does any work. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/tool-search#configure-tool-search`,children:`Configure tool search`}),` for the configurations where the fallback applies.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Use lower effort for routine tasks.`}),` Set `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`#effort-level`,children:`effort`}),` to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`\"low\"`}),` for agents that only need to read files or list directories. This reduces token usage and cost.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`For a detailed breakdown of per-feature context costs, see `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/features-overview#understand-context-costs`,children:`Understand context costs`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`sessions-and-continuity`,children:`Sessions and continuity`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`Each interaction with the SDK creates or continues a session. Capture the session ID from `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ResultMessage.session_id`}),` (available in both SDKs) to resume later. The TypeScript SDK also exposes it as a direct field on the init `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`SystemMessage`}),`; in Python it’s nested in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`SystemMessage.data`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`When you resume, the full context from previous turns is restored: files that were read, analysis that was performed, and actions that were taken. You can also fork a session to branch into a different approach without modifying the original.`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/sessions`,children:`Session management`}),` for the full guide on resume, continue, and fork patterns. To resume sessions across stateless containers or serverless hosts, pass a `,_jsxs(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/session-storage`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`session_store`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`sessionStore`}),` adapter`]}),` so the SDK mirrors transcripts to your own backend and another host can resume them. The Claude Code subprocess still writes to local disk first. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/session-storage#dual-write-architecture`,children:`Dual-write architecture`}),` for which copy outlives a fresh session versus a run resumed from the store, and how to keep the local copy ephemeral.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(Note,{children:_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`In Python, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ClaudeSDKClient`}),` handles session IDs automatically across multiple calls. See the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/python#choosing-between-query-and-claudesdkclient`,children:`Python SDK reference`}),` for details.`]})}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`handle-the-result`,children:`Handle the result`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`When the loop ends, the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ResultMessage`}),` tells you what happened and gives you the output. The `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`subtype`}),` field (available in both SDKs) is the primary way to check termination state.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Table,{children:[_jsx(_components.thead,{children:_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsx(_components.th,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Result retry`}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`center`},children:`No`})]})]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`The `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`result`}),` field holds the final text output and is only present on the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`success`}),` variant, so always check the subtype before reading it.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`All result subtypes carry `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`total_cost_usd`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`usage`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`num_turns`}),`, and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`session_id`}),` so you can track cost and resume even after errors. Guard for these cases:`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`After a session crash, the final result is an `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`error_during_execution`}),` whose cost fields may be zeroed and whose `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stop_reason`}),` is `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`null`}),`, and the process exits after emitting it. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/cost-tracking#recover-totals-after-a-session-crash`,children:`Recover totals after a session crash`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`In Python, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`total_cost_usd`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`usage`}),`, and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`model_usage`}),` are typed as optional, so check that they aren’t `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`None`}),` before you read them.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`The `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`usage`}),` field covers only the main agent loop. Use `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`modelUsage`}),`, or `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`model_usage`}),` in Python, for whole-tree token and cost accounting. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/cost-tracking`,children:`Tracking costs and usage`}),` for details on interpreting the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`usage`}),` fields.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Note,{children:[_jsx(_components.p,{children:`When a query ends on an error result:`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`A single-shot `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`query()`}),` call yields the final result message, then raises an error that includes the failure text, such as `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`Reached maximum number of turns`}),`. The raise is intentional. Wrap the loop in a try block if your code needs to continue past it. The underlying Claude Code process also exits with a nonzero code.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`A streaming input session stays alive, and you can keep sending messages, except after a session crash, which emits a final `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`error_during_execution`}),` result and exits the process.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`In TypeScript, the loop over `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`query()`}),` in a streaming input session throws when the last response ends in an error result. Wrap the loop in a try block if your code needs to continue past it.`]}),`\n`]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`The result also includes a `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stop_reason`}),` field (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`string | null`}),` in TypeScript, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`str | None`}),` in Python) indicating why the model stopped generating on its final turn. Common values are `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`end_turn`}),` (model finished normally), `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`max_tokens`}),` (hit the output token limit), and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`refusal`}),` (the model declined the request). On error results that the loop produced, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stop_reason`}),` carries the value from the last assistant response before the loop ended; the result Claude Code synthesizes after a session crash carries `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`null`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`To detect refusals, check `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stop_reason === \"refusal\"`}),` (TypeScript) or `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stop_reason == \"refusal\"`}),` (Python). See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/typescript#sdkresultmessage`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`SDKResultMessage`})}),` (TypeScript) or `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/python#resultmessage`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ResultMessage`})}),` (Python) for the full type.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`hooks`,children:`Hooks`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/hooks`,children:`Hooks`}),` are callbacks that fire at specific points in the loop: before a tool runs, after it returns, when the agent finishes, and so on. process, not inside the agent’s context window, so they don’t consume context. Hooks can also short-circuit the loop: a `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`PreToolUse`}),` hook that rejects a tool call prevents it from executing, and Claude receives the rejection message instead.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`Both SDKs support all the events above. It configures the agent with allowed tools (auto-approved so the agent runs autonomously), project settings, and safety limits on turns and reasoning effort. As the loop runs, it captures the session ID for potential resumption, handles the final result, and prints the total cost.`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`Because a single-shot
